page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81848/
Type: Talk
As at-scale AI workloads for training and inference make use of gigawatt-scale multi-GPU systems, massive data transfer, and memory access capabilities of Vera Rubin NVL72 systems in AI factories, we will show how these large systems get architected and deployed in the data center, leading to a robust reference architecture embraced globally for its speed of deployment and performance. This session will showcase internal deployments and the tools and software stack developed and leveraged on these platforms. You'll also have the opportunity to have deep dive views on how multi-node NVLink, system interconnects, and storage, as well as the latest liquid-cooled designs, are shaping the future of AI infrastructure.

In this deep dive session, learn how to use the technical architecture of CoreWeave’s RL infrastructure and inference solutions, highlighting how to define model weights, use Weights & Biases with serverless RL and the deployment of high performance inference stacks. We will cover use cases to show how to leverage these tools on NVIDIA GPUs to build a unified stack that scales seamlessly, optimizes performance, and accelerates your time to production.

Speakers: Akash Jaswal (Stripe)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82252/

Join a Stripe engineering lead for an inside look at how Stripe’s AI‑powered fraud prevention solution, Radar, is built with NVIDIA hardware and software to detect and block fraudulent transactions at massive scale, including emerging risks and new fraud vectors from agentic commerce to free trial abuse prevention. Trained on patterns derived from more than $1.4 trillion in annual payment volume, Radar uses advanced ML techniques to surface high‑confidence signals that reduced fraud rates for its users by 17% year-over-year.

### Pioneering Autonomous Science: Toward a Unified Cross-Lab Workflow
Speakers: Stephen DeWitt (Oak Ridge National Laboratory), Ian Foster (Argonne National Laboratory), Adam Thompson (NVIDIA), Bjoern Enders (NERSC), Yuliana Zamora (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81523/

This session brings together leading labs to showcase how they are using autonomous AI and high-performance computing to build scalable, reproducible data pipelines from edge to HPC. See real-world demonstrations of agentic AI automating complex, multi-facility workflows, optimizing resources, and accelerating scientific discovery with NVIDIA’s advanced frameworks. The featured labs will share insights on transparent resource management, rapid deployment, and collaborative experimentation that can be reproduced and scaled globally. Learn how these approaches unlock innovative science through seamless orchestration, portability, and reproducibility.

### Practical Context Engineering: Eliminate Bugs With High-Signal AI Code Reviews
Speakers: David Loker (CodeRabbit), Harjot Gill (CodeRabbit)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81612/
Type: Talk
AI-assisted coding has sped up code generation, but human-only code reviews are now the bottleneck. Too many pull requests (PRs), not enough reviewer bandwidth. This session shows how you can build the right context-engineering architecture that helps LLMs deliver high-signal AI code reviews. Catch logical or functional bugs, memory leaks, security vulnerabilities, code refactors, edge cases, and more. You'll see how bringing in the right context from external datasets (such as code graphs, PR history, issue tickets, MCP servers, and linters) yields better-quality reviews of PR diffs. You will leave with a practical playbook to pilot and scale AI code reviews that increase your release velocity with fewer bugs.

### Push the Performance Frontier of Computer Vision Models With NVFP4 Reduced Precision
Speakers: Leo Du (NVIDIA), Pradeep Ramachandran (KLA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81547/
Type: Talk
Reduced-precision formats like NVFP4 are shaping the future of AI by enabling faster training and inference without compromising model accuracy. In this talk, we share best practices, insights, and lessons learned from training and deploying convolutional neural network (CNN) and transformer models using advanced reduced-precision techniques while maintaining full accuracy relative to high-precision baselines. We'll explore how reduced precision, combined with optimized CUDA kernels, delivers significant speedups for both training and inference—pushing the performance frontier of computer vision workloads on modern GPUs. This methodology can be broadly applied across industrial use cases requiring highly accurate, low-latency computer vision models, including manufacturing, medical imaging, autonomous vehicles, robotics, and more.

### Quantum and HPC at Riken: The Sum is Greater Than Its Parts
Speakers: Satoshi Matsuoka (Riken), Mitsuhisa Sato (Riken)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81686/
Type: Talk
Discover how we realize the vision in quantum-HPC integration that "the sum is greater than its parts" by integrating the Fugaku supercomputer and NVIDIA GB200 clusters with superconducting and ion-trap QPUs in our JHPC-Quantum project. Our tightly coupled software stack, including the low-latency RPC middleware, orchestrates heterogeneous resources, and when combined with our novel quantum-HPC hybrid algorithms, we achieve utility-scale results in realistic problems in areas such as advanced quantum chemistry and computational fluid dyamics using hybrid algorithms—not just those that are advantageous to quantum. With rapid advances in both quantum and HPC systems, these will likely lead to true quantum advantages in real applications in the very near future.

### Real-time Voice Transformation with GPU Accelerated AI
Speakers: James Maki (NVIDIA), Carter Huffman (Modulate)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81744/
Type: Talk
Modulate’s VoiceWear enables real-time voice transformation, demanding high-fidelity audio and near-zero latency. This session details how Modulate and NVIDIA migrated critical VoiceWear workloads from CPU to GPU, significantly reducing latency within their streaming pipeline. We’ll explore the process of identifying bottlenecks, the impact of precision and framework selection on performance, and how targeted CUDA and inference-stack optimizations delivered measurable gains. Attendees will gain practical strategies for accelerating sequential, real-time ML workloads for production.

### Redefining Scalable AI Performance: OCI Supercomputing in the Cloud (Presented by Oracle)
Speakers: Jagwinder Brar (Oracle), Taylor Newill (Oracle)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82389/

Organizations building or scaling frontier AI models can’t be constrained by infrastructure. In this session, discover how Oracle Cloud Infrastructure (OCI) connects hundreds of thousands of NVIDIA GPUs. Oracle’s AI superclusters deliver up to 16 zettaFLOPS of peak performance, giving you the scale, low-latency GPU-to-GPU communication, and price-performance required for the largest AI training and inference workloads. In this session you will learn how OCI’s Acceleron ultra-low-latency RoCE networking and NVIDIA’s next-generation GPU instances dramatically increase LLM throughput, accelerate multimodal and scientific workloads, and improve cluster utilization, so you can train faster, deploy sooner, and operate more efficiently. Walk away with a clear understanding of how to move from experimentation to industrialized AI at global scale, with the performance, reliability, and sovereignty controls enterprise AI demands.

### Superior Tokenomics for AI Factories: Building Cost-Efficient Inference at Scale
Speakers: Kevin Deierling (NVIDIA), Kunal Anand (F5, Inc.)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81794/

The next wave of enterprise innovation hinges on successfully deploying large language models with predictable cost and performance. This session demystifies the token economy and highlights why the critical, yet often overlooked, network and security infrastructure layer, together with the accelerated Intelligent AI Proxy, has become essential for Gen AI success. Learn how this infrastructure layer enables two fundamental shifts: • Intelligent Traffic Management to Optimize Tokens, the Currency of AI Factories: How you can increase output token throughput by more than 50% and drive significantly higher return on investment from their AI factories. • Disaggregated Inference, a Critical Architectural Shift for Token Efficiency: How you can significantly improve GPU utilization and enable a far more scalable enterprise AI factories, delivering up to 3X increase in inference performance.

### Telecom Special Address: The AI Grid—Intelligently Connecting AI Infrastructure
Speakers: Ronnie Vasishta (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82003/
Type: Talk
The unsatiable demand for AI computing is creating the need for many new kinds of AI infrastructure providers. As AI inference traffic scales, compute must move beyond a few centralized data centers to a vast, distributed AI grid. Telcos, with their nationwide networks and dense footprint of data centers, are uniquely positioned to transform existing infrastructure into this scaled AI grid. This talk explores how telcos can build an AI grid to power new AI services and applications, gives a first look at NVIDIA’s reference architecture, highlights edge AI applications that run best on this grid, and shares announcements from partners already on this journey. It also offers a forward-looking view of how innovations in AI-RAN, autonomous networks, and 6G come together as workloads on the AI grid—creating a once-in-a-decade opportunity for telcos to redefine their role in the AI era.

As automakers move to AI-enabled, data-driven development pipelines, they’re unlocking new levels of efficiency, quality, and innovation across the vehicle life cycle. They’ll share real examples of AI-powered design, automated engineering, factory optimization, and robotics training converging into a unified end-to-end pipeline, along with insights on the technologies and collaboration driving industry transformation.

### Agentic AI Using Open Source Models: Finetuning, Deployment and Chip Design Case Studies
Speakers: Chris Alexiuk (NVIDIA), David Loker (CodeRabbit), Shraddha Sridhar (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81702/
Type: Tutorial
Open models have evolved from experimental alternatives to production-grade building blocks for agentic AI and enterprise applications. But deciding when to use open source models, and choosing and customizing them requires a clear framework. In this session, we'll walk through a practical decision framework for deploying open models at scale from picking the right model size for your agentic tasks to the software and practices for customizing, evaluating, and deploying them. We'll bring it to life with two case studies: how CodeRabbit runs open-source models in production, and how NVIDIA’s chip design team turned early failures into a repeatable flywheel: using a decision framework to pick use cases, then fine-tuning open-source models for the patterns that stuck. Whether you're building agentic AI, internal copilots, or cost-sensitive services, this talk will give you the framework and tools to ship with confidence.

In this session, GIGABYTE (Giga Computing) will reveal how we are bridging the gap between cutting-edge silicon and a successful industrial-scale deployment with RIKEN QHPC project in running NVIDIA Blackwell products. We will explore the architectural evolution of our latest server lineup, specifically engineered to harness the power of NVIDIA Vera CPUs and Rubin GPUs.

### Building Frontier AI Models for eCommerce with the NVIDIA AI Factory
Speakers: Peter Wu (Shopee), Anxiang Zeng (Shopee)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81452/

Large-model technologies are rapidly transforming ecommerce. See how our model powers traffic distribution, search, content generation, and customer service to enhance efficiency and user experience, while our AI Merchant Bot improves seller efficiency and drives tangible growth. We'll highlight breakthrough search capabilities of large models in ecommerce search, including query rewriting, attribute enrichment, semantic retrieval, and multi-modal search, and how they significantly improve search quality and user intent understanding.

Learn how IBM Fusion and watsonx integrate with NVIDIA’s platform to eliminate data bottlenecks, streamline pipelines, and enable secure, scalable deployments. Also hear from Liqiang Wang, BioHPC Director at UT Southwestern, on how the medical center is deploying next-generation AI infrastructure with IBM Fusion and NVIDIA’s AI Data Platform to train larger models, process complex datasets, and power advanced research - from accelerated drug discovery to AI-driven clinical training and improved researcher productivity.

Speakers: Michael Balint (NVIDIA), William Benton (NVIDIA)

Whether you're launching your first AI initiative or modernizing your infrastructure, this session provides the technical blueprint and best practices you need to operationalize AI at scale.

### NVIDIA Vera: Reimagining CPUs for Agents, Reasoning, and What’s Next
Speakers: Ian Finder (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82437/
Type: Talk
As AI workflows expand across training, inference, analytics, and reasoning-driven applications, CPUs play a growing role in AI factory performance—from orchestration and application execution to compute- and memory-intensive processing. This session introduces NVIDIA Vera, a next-generation CPU architecture built for these demands. We'll cover the workload trends shaping CPU design and key Vera technologies, including the custom Olympus core, high-bandwidth LPDDR5X memory subsystem, and NVIDIA Scalable Coherency Fabric. Learn how Vera's architecture is designed to maximize CPU performance and efficiency across the AI factory.

### Operationalize Agentic AI at Hyperscale: Microsoft Foundry in Azure AI Factories (Presented by Microsoft)
Speakers: Nitin Nagarkatte (Microsoft), Anand Raman (Microsoft), Vipul Modi (Microsoft)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82399/
Type: Talk
As enterprises move from AI experimentation to always‑on, agentic systems, the challenge shifts from accessing models to operating intelligent agents reliably at scale. In this session, Microsoft will demonstrate what goes into developing purpose-built AI factories using NVIDIA technology to run production‑grade agentic AI workloads. We begin with the design principles behind Microsoft’s AI factory approach—how NVIDIA GPU architectures are translated into deployable, repeatable platforms within Azure, for long‑running, multi‑step agent workflows across thousands of GPUs, and heterogeneous networks. See how agent behavior at scale reshapes system design, where performance, reliability, and operations must work together to keep agents responsive and continuously available. Then we'll dive deeper into Microsoft Foundry as the control plane for agentic AI. Foundry enables teams to discover, deploy, and operate frontier and open models, including NVIDIA Nemotron reasoning models and NIM microservices, and turn them into AI agents. By unifying model catalogs, agent services, governance, and infrastructure, Microsoft Foundry and NVIDIA help enterprises move from single‑prompt interactions to coordinated, multi‑agent systems in production. We conclude with an inside look at how Microsoft operates its global NVIDIA fleet through Foundry to deliver predictable performance, security, and cost efficiency, transforming AI factories into production platforms that power enterprise‑grade agentic AI at scale.

### Physical AI for the Real World: A Vision From NVIDIA Robotics Research
Speakers: Yashraj Narang (NVIDIA), Jim Fan (NVIDIA), Yuke Zhu (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81479/
Type: Talk
One of the hardest problems in physical AI is transforming robots from task-specific machines to general-purpose, intelligent collaborators. NVIDIA Research is pioneering a unified AI- and simulation-driven approach to solve this challenge, working from theoretical advances all the way to high-performance deployment. Join this session to learn about research breakthroughs in sim-to-real, real-world learning, model architecture, and model training, as well as key milestones on the path to generalist embodied intelligence for real-world applications. Gain perspectives about where robotics is headed, what technical hurdles remain, and how the next wave of research innovation could unlock entirely new markets.

### Real-Time Generative Video Re-Styling for Gaming in the Next-Generation Datacenter
Speakers: Ethan Petersen (Decart), Bhavani Gopalakrishna Rao (NVIDIA), Alex Dunn (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81596/

Decart’s Lucy Restyle is a World Transformation Model that reimagines any video, game, or camera feed into a new digital world in real time. We will dive into how Decart and NVIDIA optimized Lucy Restyle inference for low-latency, cost-efficient cloud deployment on NVIDIA B40 GPUs through GeForce NOW (GFN). Finally, we’ll demonstrate how to harness Lucy Restyle to Lucy Restyle completely restyle titles via the ultimate modding platform: RTX Remix.

### Scale Inference Using Open Models: How Nebius Token Factory Delivers Control and Efficiency (Presented by Nebius)

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82234/

AI is moving from experimentation to global production, and inference efficiency and quality are the defining competitive edge. This session dives deep into the engineering behind high-performance, open-model inference, covering the system decisions, runtime techniques, and architectural patterns that unlock predictable, real-world performance at scale. Learn how Nebius Token Factory and NVIDIA push beyond benchmark-driven results to deliver structural, compounding gains in latency, throughput, and cost.

Speakers: Woosuk Kwon (Inferact)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82059/
Type: Talk
As LLMs grow in size, context length, and architectural complexity, vLLM must evolve to meet new performance and scalability challenges. This talk presents key improvements in vLLM's core architecture, including a GPU-first design for zero CPU overheads and an architecture for cluster-scale serving deployment. It also highlights major optimizations in KV cache management and GPU kernels. Gain a detailed technical view of how vLLM is advancing to deliver next-level performance on NVIDIA GPUs at scale.

Speakers: Greg Forrest (Lockheed Martin)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-ex82257/

As AI systems move from experimentation to operational deployment, organizations in aerospace, energy, manufacturing, and the public sector face a common challenge: how to deploy AI reliably in environments where failure, data leakage, or downtime is unacceptable. This session explores how AI factory architectures are being designed to support high-assurance, mission-critical workloads across on-premises, edge, disconnected, and regulated environments.

### Eliminate the Data Bottleneck: Accelerate Enterprise AI at Scale (Presented by Elastic)
Speakers: James Williams (Elastic)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-ex82307/
Type: Theater Talk
Successful enterprise AI deployments require context from your petabytes of unstructured, proprietary company data. Yet indexing and vectorizing this data at enterprise speed and scale remains one of the biggest bottlenecks to implementing AI in production, resulting in stalled deployments and high overhead costs. Elastic’s GPU-accelerated search with NVIDIA cuVS now delivers 12x faster indexing speeds and enables enterprise-scale AI deployments that accelerate your path from proof of concept to production.

### AI Grid for Robots: Applications and Infrastructure for Physical AI Powered by Telcos
Speakers: Ryuji Wakikawa (SoftBank), Grant Ries (T-Mobile)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82014/

Physical AI is moving robots from labs into streets, offices, factories, and public spaces—where they must see, think, and act in milliseconds. Traditional cloud-centric architectures can’t meet the latency, reliability, and resiliency demands of these real-world uses, making a distributed AI grid essential. Two leading telecom operators will showcase physical AI use cases, demonstrating how AI-enhanced radio networks and distributed computing let robots offload heavy perception and decision workloads while maintaining real-time responsiveness and safety. The session will outline key architectural building blocks of an AI-native network, explore its evolution toward 6G, and explain why telecom AI grids are poised to become the default fabric for the next wave of physical AI.

### AI Research Breakthroughs from NVIDIA Research (Hosted by Karoly of Two Minute Papers)

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81810/

They'll dive into the latest breakthroughs in AI, spotlight the most promising emerging technical trends, and candidly explore the biggest open challenges facing the field today. Join us for an interactive, high-energy conversation packed with cutting-edge insights!

### Accelerate Hybrid Quantum-Classical Research at Scale With NVIDIA CUDA-Q (Presented by Foxconn)
Speakers: Min Hsiu Hsieh (Hon Hai Technology Group (Foxconn))
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82043/
Type: Talk
This talk explains why Hon Hai Research Institute’s integrated strategy for advancing quantum science across the full stack requires an integrated strategy, developing both better hardware and applications algorithms together. We'll outline Hon Hai Research Institute’s approach to this—including Foxconn’s ion-trap roadmap—from first-generation blade-type traps to the establishment of Taiwan’s first industry-based ion-trap quantum computing laboratory and the creation of multi-layer and chip-based architectures. We'll also highlight advancements from Hon Hai Research Institute in software and theory for areas including quantum error correction, efficient tomography and noise benchmarking, quantum machine learning, and quantum networking.

### Accelerated AI Networking with DOCA
Speakers: Alexander Petrovskiy (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81857/
Type: Talk
As AI models scale, data center networking must provide ultra-low latency, high throughput, and programmable data paths so training and inference workloads remain efficient. With the NVIDIA DOCA software platform, developers can move critical data-path functions onto NVIDIA BlueField data processing units (DPUs) using DOCA libraries and microservices to offload, accelerate, and isolate cloud infrastructure while enhancing observability. We’ll cover how DOCA enables centralized orchestration of BlueField‑accelerated services, dynamic traffic steering, and fine‑grained network and security controls that work together to deliver secure, high‑performance fabrics with predictable performance and efficient resource utilization for multi‑tenant AI environments at scale.

Learn how developers build and adapt domain-specific models, operationalize them using SAP GenAI hub and NVIDIA NIM, and apply them through SAP Joule for Developers to tackle complex challenges like code explanation and cloud migration. The session closes with a forward-looking view on agentic and physical AI, where live business context connects digital intelligence to real-world execution.

### Building Safer Physical AI: Certification, Cybersecurity, and Agentic Control
Speakers: Riccardo Mariani (NVIDIA), Akshay Sharan (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82462/

Safety, cybersecurity, and AI safety are interconnected mission-critical properties of physical AI. As autonomous systems move into the real world, they require a foundation of trust spanning from sensors and hardware platforms to operating systems, reasoning models, and agentic functions. Join us to explore the NVIDIA Halos safety framework and learn how to architect certifiable systems for Level 4 autonomous vehicles and robotics. We’ll examine the design of robust hardware and software safety foundations—integrating hardware platform and operating system—to ensure rigorous safety and systematic cybersecurity by design across the entire stack. You'll learn how to leverage NVIDIA DRIVE Hyperion for automotive, and NVIDIA IGX for robotics, to implement advanced inside-out and outside-in functional safety use cases for scalable autonomy.

Moderated by Richard Kerris, general manager of Media2 at NVIDIA, former CTO of Lucasfilm, and previous global lead for Apple’s entertainment industry developer ecosystem, the discussion will offer candid insights and real-world use cases on how collaboration between tech and culture leaders is defining the next era of entertainment.

### Continuous Inference Benchmarking of the AI Frontier
Speakers: Cameron Quilici (SemiAnalysis), Dylan Patel (SemiAnalysis), Kimbo Chen (SemiAnalysis)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82474/
Type: Talk
As large language model architectures evolve, traditional peak performance metrics are becoming less predictive of real-world efficiency. Effective inference benchmarking now requires a multidimensional approach that balances raw throughput against energy consumption, cost-per-token, and the critical "interactivity" thresholds required for user-facing applications. This session introduces InferenceX, SemiAnalysis’ open-source benchmark suite designed to navigate this complexity by sweeping the latest software optimizations across leading AI accelerators. It moves beyond static datasheets to examine nightly, reproducible results that reveal how frontier models perform under variable production workloads. Learn how the InferenceX benchmarks are designed, how to run them, and how to interpret the results to make informed decisions.

Speakers: Yineng Zhang (Together AI)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82026/

This session presents a practical workflow for profiling inference stacks, identifying real bottlenecks, and evaluating optimizations across kernels, scheduling, and system design, showing how to leverage fast-moving open-source innovations while applying our own enhancements to achieve production-grade performance and stability, and how to feed lessons from production back into upstream development.

The focus is now on “doing AI right” for real impact. Top VCs now treat NVIDIA partnership as essential portfolio infrastructure—not just for compute, but as a strategic accelerator that helps founders solve hard problems, lead technically, and speed up R&D. This session shares how startups used NVIDIA’s ecosystem to achieve breakthrough results.

We'll also explore what it takes to build state-of-the-art models—from data preparation and quality assurance to large-scale distributed training, compression optimization, and deployment (using tools such as NeMo, Model Optimizer, Dynamo, TRT-LLM)—as well as how to develop a successful developer ecosystem around your models and products to ensure long-term sustainability.

Through real-world examples and a short demo, we will show how AI coding fits into existing IT workflows instead of requiring new tools or large integrations. The focus is on practical and low-risk use cases that deliver quick value, reduce ongoing maintenance work, and help teams spend more time on higher-impact efforts like modernization and optimization.

Speakers: Shantanu Sarangi (NVIDIA), Mahmut Yilmaz (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81951/

AI factories are transforming AI workloads, but maintaining uptime in these GPU-powered environments is a critical challenge—where even a single fault can disrupt weeks of progress and cost millions. Learn how NVIDIA’s advanced fault detection, predictive analytics, and innovative In-System Test (IST) facilitate rapid diagnostics, enabling organizations to quickly identify and resolve issues. By minimizing downtime and preventing costly interruptions, these technologies drive a significant reduction in total cost of ownership and deliver improved return on investment. This session will highlight how automated recovery and hierarchical diagnostics empower organizations to deploy and scale AI infrastructure with confidence and efficiency.

The next leap in enterprise AI is industrializing intelligence, where automation and human input combine to improve agility, decision quality, and process efficiency. This session shows how NVIDIA applies lean manufacturing principles and agentic AI to its own AI operations through three core MLOps platforms: the Data Factory for synthetic data generation and labeling, the Eval Factory for model benchmarking, and the NIM Factory for inference optimization. From these real-world examples, we distill a practical playbook for representing any process in AI-native terms, identifying high-impact automation opportunities, embedding continuous improvement, and building a culture of operational excellence. The result is an AI-first organization with a repeatable model for AI-enhanced operations, one that extends beyond MLOps to transform the entire enterprise and its business model.

### Shatter the Memory Wall: Composable Building Blocks for Massive Scale Analytics
Speakers: Felipe Aramburu (NVIDIA), Rodrigo Aramburu (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81873/
Type: Talk
As GPU-accelerated analytics scale to terabytes and beyond, memory management and observability become critical infrastructure. We introduce a composable, engine-agnostic approach to shattering GPU memory limits and understanding query-level resource consumption. We'll deep-dive into cuCascade, a library for memory reservation and topology discovery that prevents out-of-memory failures by gracefully spilling data between GPU, host, and disk memory tiers. We'll also introduce a semantic telemetry layer for always-on profiling, enabling developers to visualize query plans and resource throughput across GPUs in real time. We demonstrate both tools working together inside Sirius, NVIDIA's GPU-native analytics engine, showing real telemetry output and memory tier management on live workloads. Learn how these composable building blocks help engine developers identify bandwidth bottlenecks, optimize memory utilization, and push toward speed-of-light analytics performance.

They will also provide a technical deep dive into their deployment strategy for high-performance, real-time inference.

### Accelerate AI Inference Using DOCA for Storage
Speakers: Oren Duer (NVIDIA), Omri Kahalon (NVIDIA), Ariel Kit (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81773/

Real-time AI inference at scale requires high-performance GPUs combined with efficient data movement, preprocessing, and data access from edge to cloud. Developers often struggle to eliminate bottlenecks that slow down inference pipelines and waste valuable compute cycles. The NVIDIA DOCA software platform enables high-performance AI inference processing by accelerating data movement and preprocessing tasks using RDMA, NIXL, and GPUNetIO. DOCA-powered storage solutions like the NVIDIA AI Data Platform design and S3-compatible storage enable seamless, high-performance data access for AI workloads. This session demonstrates how to design inference services that deliver responsive and reliable results for latency-sensitive applications in both AI factory and edge environments. It also shows how to apply DOCA-accelerated networking and storage for high-performance data access.

### Empower US Manufacturing Through Wistron's Smart Manufacturing (Presented by Wistron Corp.)
Speakers: John Lu (Wistron Corp.)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82050/

Join Wistron technology leaders to explore how the company’s digital twin platform, built on NVIDIA Omniverse libraries and OpenUSD, is accelerating factory deployment, from Hsinchu to Dallas. Discover how these factory-scale digital twins are enabling many use cases; including streamlined factory design, layout validation, and manufacturing process simulation in the early planning. Get a deep dive into how these digital twins are enabling virtual commissioning and helping identify integration issues among robots, conveyors, and automation systems before physical installation. Learn about the real business outcomes being generated across Wistron’s operations, from faster iteration cycles to fewer deployment risks and eliminated rework during ramp-up.

### Five Bottlenecks, Five Fixes: How We Avoid Leaving Training Performance on the Table
Speakers: Sylvain Gugger (Jane Street), Corwin de Zahr (Jane Street)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82065/
Type: Talk
GPU clusters are fast, until they're not. We'll walk through five performance pitfalls from real researcher code, the fixes that made them disappear, and how to use technologies like CUDA streams, CUDA graphs, and custom kernels along the way.

### From Grace to Vera: NVIDIA’s next data center CPU
Speakers: Matthias Langer (NVIDIA), Lukas Alt (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81680/
Type: Talk
In the past two years, the NVIDIA Grace CPU has reshaped the foundation of modern data centers. Its successor, the NVIDIA Vera CPU, will become available soon. Vera marks an exciting evolution of CPU design. Based on a custom Arm CPU core, this new CPU provides unprecedented performance and efficiency. But what does this mean for system integrators and developers? In this talk, we will discuss how Vera improves upon Grace. We'll analyze the new features of the upcoming Vera CPU, and how you can use them to unleash Vera’s full potential. We will also revisit typical applications and usage scenarios, and detail how Vera’s architecture is particularly geared toward speeding up such workloads. After the session, join us at our follow on "Connect with the Experts Session" to ask further questions with our team of experts - How to Run and Optimize Your Workloads on the NVIDIA Grace and Vera CPUs [CWES81771] .

### From Prototype to Playable: Bringing Scalable ML Into "World of Tanks: HEAT"
Speakers: Dmitry Nozhnin (Wargaming Group), Dora Csillag (NVIDIA), Andrii Alieksieienko (Wargaming Group)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81622/
Type: Talk
This session explores how World of Tanks: HEAT plans to transition ML from research notebooks to live combat using NVIDIA Triton. We begin by defining the "strategic recommendation system" within a gaming context—covering data prep, model selection, and player-centric evaluation. From there, we dive into the infrastructure: why traditional in-process inference fails in high-intensity battles and how Triton standardizes deployment across CPU and GPU. Learn how we utilize ONNX, TensorRT, and dynamic batching to optimize high-frequency models and use Triton’s Model Analyzer to ensure peak performance before players hit the battlefield.

### Holistic Model Co-Design: Close the Loop With Software and Hardware for Inference Efficiency
Speakers: Ritchie Zhao (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82154/
Type: Talk
The economics of inference are changing faster than ever, with the cost of serving frontier models growing at an unprecedented rate. To continue advancing intelligence per unit cost, we must look beyond isolated optimizations and adopt a holistic, cross-layer design philosophy spanning silicon, software, and model architecture. This session will explore the opportunities of hardware/software/algorithm co-design, highlighting how aligning model innovations with NVIDIA hardware features can unlock orders-of-magnitude efficiency gains. By educating the community on these co-design principles, we can accelerate the development of the next generation of frontier models and deepen our strategic advantage through tighter integration between hardware and intelligence.

### How Open World Models are Powering the Next Breakthroughs in Physical AI
Speakers: Ming-Yu Liu (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81667/
Type: Talk
Generating the photorealistic data and robust reasoning capabilities needed for machines to understand real-world complexity remains one of the biggest bottlenecks in physical AI. NVIDIA Cosmos world foundation models are at the core of AI models and agents that can perceive, understand, and adapt to the real-world like never before. Learn how developers can use Cosmos open models and tools to accelerate training, customize pipelines, and deploy faster across robotics, autonomous vehicles, and vision AI agents. This session will also explore how the latest Cosmos updates advance physics-aware learning, unlock simulation-to-real capabilities, and enable long-running agents and frameworks like OpenClaw to orchestrate continuous synthetic data generation and reasoning workflows at scale.

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82341/

Few environments have greater demand for rapid, relevant, and reliable operations than Formula One. In this session, you’ll get privileged visibility into how AI-ready hardware is powering high-performance workloads across the uniquely distributed, time-critical operations of Formula One racing. The session will also cover best practices for optimizing inferencing across enterprise environments, based on Lenovo AI inferencing infrastructure, workstations, and services and real-world AI use case deployments with NVIDIA.

### Leverage Physical AI to Simulate and Orchestrate Robotic Fleets for Retail Fulfillment Centers
Speakers: Martin Umland (Otto Group), Stefan Borsutzky (Otto Group one.O GmbH)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81730/
Type: Talk
Dive into the technology behind the new era of physical AI that's redefining Europe's supply chain and facility automation. Otto Group presents the “robotic coordination layer,” which connects robot fleets in real warehouses to a digital twin, enabling the deployment of the latest technology for intelligent automation. Discover how Otto Groups, NVIDIA, and Reply collaborated to not only launch the latest AI and robotics systems in the field of logistics across Europe, but also create a scalable blueprint for the future of logistics.

### Liquid Cooling: How to Achieve 100% Heat Capture on 500kW+ Racks (Presented by CoolIT Systems)
Speakers: Andrew Buckrell (CoolIT Systems)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82004/
Type: Talk
As rack power densities surge beyond 100 kilowatts (kW), achieving 100% heat capture becomes increasingly essential for thermal management and operational efficiency. Current coldplate designs focus on high-thermal design power components such as CPUs and GPUs, but future architectures demand complete heat removal across all components. Even at 90% heat capture, a 500kW rack leaves 50kW for air cooling, which is a stretch for even the best air-cooled implementations. Enabling 100% heat capture will be critical for supporting next-generation AI infrastructure. Join us to learn how integrated cooling loops and advanced coldplate technologies enable complete heat removal across the entire server.

### Post-Training Nemotron With RL: Multi‑Environment Training for Reasoning and Tool-Using Agents
Speakers: Oleksii Kuchaiev (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81558/
Type: Talk
How do we move beyond simple instruction-following to models that truly reason, plan, and use tools? The answer lies in the rigor of the post-training pipeline. In this session, we unveil the technology behind Nemotron’s reasoning and tool use capabilities. While we will cover the essentials of imitation learning and synthetic data generation, the spotlight will be on the frontier of reinforcement learning (RL). We will demonstrate how multi-environment RL training serves as the catalyst for unlocking multi-step reasoning and robust tool use. By analyzing the interplay between reward modeling, reinforcement learning from human feedback, and environment-aware training, we will show how Nemotron achieves superior performance in complex agentic workflows. Join us to explore the specific techniques NVIDIA uses to align models not just for chat, but for cognitive action.

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-ex82041/

As AI workloads continue to grow in size, power density, and complexity, data centers must evolve beyond traditional architectures. This session explores how PEGATRON is enabling next‑generation AI factories through automated manufacturing and tightly integrated server platforms. From accelerated rack‑level deployment to end‑to‑end infrastructure integration, PEGATRON delivers a comprehensive solution spanning compute, networking, and storage—engineered to interconnect racks and clusters in modern AI data centers.

### Provide the Best Care for Patients Using Foundation Models and Agents
Speakers: Raghav Mani (NVIDIA), Phil Lindemann (Epic)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81805/

The next round of innovation in healthcare is being defined by the fusion of foundation models and intelligent agents, creating systems that learn, reason, and act alongside clinicians to deliver truly personalized medicine. In this session, we explore how AI is transforming the very fabric of healthcare delivery by embedding reasoning systems directly into the flow of care. Agentic AI systems, built on these models, interpret, anticipate, and act—surfacing insights at the right moment, summarizing encounters, and coordinating care across disciplines. The result: care that is faster, safer, and more human. This talk will show how Epic is using the NVIDIA AI platform in making this future real—combining accelerated computing and specialized medical AI models and agents to enable intelligent, closed-loop healthcare systems.

### Securing Intelligence: A Practical Framework for AI and LLM Security (Presented by TrendAI)
Speakers: Fernando Cardoso (TrendAI)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-ex82075/

As AI and large language models move from experimentation into mission-critical systems, organizations are building intelligent platforms to power decision-making, automation, and innovation at scale, creating a new security reality. From data pipelines and model development to deployment, inference, and real-world impact, AI systems expand the attack surface in ways traditional security can't address. We'll explore a practical Security for AI Framework, aligned with the OWASP Top 10 for LLMs, and see how modern threats like adversarial manipulation, data poisoning, prompt injection, model abuse, and synthetic media attacks are reshaping the landscape. Learn how organizations are embedding governance, access control, validation, monitoring, and response into their AI platforms and AI factory architectures, turning security into a foundation for trusted, production-ready intelligence.

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81851/

We’ll outline the full-stack infrastructure and software requirements of an AI factory and demonstrate how NVIDIA’s NV-Certified systems, enterprise reference architectures, validated designs, and exemplar AI factories distill our learnings from real-world deployments into repeatable patterns that help system partners accelerate implementation with consistency, performance, and efficiency.

### The Earth System at 1 km Resolution: Breaking Frontiers in Climate Science
Speakers: Daniel Klocke (Max Planck Institute for Meteorology (MPI-M))
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82185/
Type: Talk
We present the first-ever global simulation of the full Earth system at an unprecedented 1 km resolution—a milestone recognized with the 2025 Gordon Bell Prize in Climate Modeling. Led by researchers from the Max Planck Institute for Meteorology and the German Climate Computing Center, this simulation captures the dynamic flow of energy, water, and carbon across the atmosphere, ocean, and land, unlocking new frontiers in climate science. The team harnessed the full computational power of JUPITER (the world’s largest GH200 Superchip installation) and ALPS systems, building on a long-standing collaboration with NVIDIA to advance the ICON codebase. The heterogeneous architecture, balancing Earth system components on both Grace CPUs and Hopper GPUs, enabled innovative optimization and acceleration techniques, reducing the code complexity by half while increasing performance and portability.

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-ex82291/

Cloudflare provides a serverless platform for building agents that can run code, execute tools, and persist state without managing servers or backend infrastructure. In this session, we’ll show how workers, durable objects, and Cloudflare’s Agents SDK can power a self-learning agent by having the agent control a car to go around obstacles in a simulated environment. This illustrates how Cloudflare enables agents that can learn, adapt, and evolve over time, rather than operate as stateless chat interfaces.

### Unlocking AI Performance​ with CXL-Powered KV Cache​ (Presented by Penguin Solutions)
Speakers: Phil Pokorny (Penguin Solutions)

CXL memory can play a transformative role by expanding local and shared infrastructure to save processed results, enabling GPUs to focus on more high-value tasks. Learn how sharing cached work across multiple GPUs not only accelerates response times, but also unlocks new functionalities, driving higher system efficiency and reducing the total cost of inference. Attend this talk to explore the future of AI inference optimization through the benefits of KV cache.

### Capital Group, Global Asset Manager, Transforms Investor onboarding with NVIDIA and Infosys AI Next platform (Presented by Infosys)

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-ex82327/

Capital Group Investor Onboarding Transformation: Infosys modernized and deployed a portfolio guidelines analysis solution on Infosys AI Next platform, powered by NVIDIA NIM for Capital Group. This AI-based solution streamlines the review of compliance language to speed up client onboarding, enable analysis and research, and reduce reputational and financial risk. The solution automatically extracts compliance language from investment guidelines documents, categorizes it, and maps the language to applicable compliance rules. This implementation reduces manual work and enables portfolio guidelines team to review and approve onboarding of clients with significantly higher speed and precision. The platform, in production today, has increased the efficiency of review and processing volume significantly across customers’ onboarding workflows.

### The Data Storage Platform That Powers AI Factories (Presented by Everpure)
Speakers: Erin Stevens (Everpure)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-ex82309/
Type: Theater Talk
Ready to move your AI pilot from proof of concept to production scale? Your success depends on an AI factory architecture that continuously ingests data, refines models, and delivers inference at scale. And while GPUs power the AI factory engine, your data storage platform determines how fast and consistently your AI factory actually runs. This session explores why your storage strategy is critical to AI success—and how Everpure delivers the performance, scalability, and reliability AI Factories demand. Learn how the Everpure AI Data Platform, including FlashBlade, FlashStack for AI, and Data Stream, accelerates every stage of the pipeline from data preparation through training and inference.

### 2025 Gordon Bell Winner: Forecasting Tsunamis in Real Time With Digital Twins
Speakers: Omar Ghattas (The University of Texas at Austin), Stefan Henneking (The University of Texas at Austin)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82161/
Type: Talk
The Cascadia subduction zone is capable of unleashing a magnitude 9 earthquake with tsunamis as high as 30 meters, endangering millions in the Pacific Northwest. While it has been quiet since 1700, scientists place a 37% probability of an 8.2+ earthquake in the next 50 years. In response, the 2025 Gordon Bell Prize was awarded to UT-Austin, UC San Diego, and Lawrence Livermore National Laboratory for the world’s first digital twin for real-time, full-physics-based tsunami early warning. Leveraging exascale-class HPC systems, it delivers unprecedented accuracy and speed, achieving a ten-billion-fold performance improvement over existing state-of-the-art methods. This enables rapid, data-driven hazard assessment and actionable insights during critical windows. The work not only redefines the boundaries of real-time tsunami forecasting, but also sets a new standard for disaster resilience.

### Accelerate Enterprise AI Adoption: Secure Your Agentic Foundations from Day 1 (Presented by CrowdStrike)
Speakers: Mike Petronaci (CrowdStrike)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82266/

Enterprise AI adoption is accelerating through AI agents embedded in applications and workflows. While these agents drive innovation, they present unique security challenges as organizations move to production. Traditional security approaches weren't built for agent architectures, creating vulnerabilities in agent logic, prompts, model interactions, and infrastructure. Learn how CrowdStrike is collaborating with NVIDIA to drive secure enterprise AI adoption across the entire AI life cycle, from governing agent behavior to protecting models, and delivering built-in protection for AI factories. By integrating security into agent design and runtime, organizations can confidently scale AI systems while managing risk.

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82191/

We’ll discuss how we assess and improve the performance of quantum computing hardware, the expanding role for HPC/GPUs/AI in this quest, and when it will (and won’t!) be useful to integrate HPC and GPUs with fault-tolerant quantum computers.

### Build Secure AI Infrastructure With DOCA
Speakers: Ofir Arkin (NVIDIA), Ariel Kit (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81625/
Type: Talk
As AI factories continue to evolve, cybersecurity must protect infrastructure, applications, and emerging agentic workflows without consuming critical compute resources or impacting tokenomics. The NVIDIA DOCA software platform addresses these challenges with a suite of security microservices, frameworks, and APIs that advance protection without compromising performance. See how DOCA delivers accelerated, programmable runtime security for AI infrastructure. Powered by NVIDIA BlueField, DOCA enforces security at line rate without impacting computing performance, installing host-based software, or integrating directly into protected workloads. Learn how to apply granular network security policies at line rate, detect and block threats to AI workloads in real time, implement prompt-level defenses, and secure agentic workflows, the new frontier of AI security.

This effort introduces a collaborative, open platform built on NVIDIA’s AI infrastructure, leveraging Isaac for Healthcare for photorealistic surgical simulation. It establishes the first unified framework tailored to surgery robotics, featuring modular robot control, multi-modal perception (vision and kinematics), and vision-language-action (VLA) pipelines—all designed for real-world deployment on accelerated computing platforms. Researchers worldwide can now generate high-fidelity synthetic datasets, train policies via imitation and reinforcement learning, and validate algorithms against shared benchmarks enabling reproducible, scalable research in open surgery automation for the first time.

In this forward-looking conversation, sector leaders from industrial products, energy, and technology will explore how organizations can prioritize their accelerated computing resources across competing use cases. Rather than relying on static forecasts, the discussion will introduce a practical framework for aligning GPU deployment with business value, operational resilience, and broader societal needs. The goal is not simply to manage existing resources, but to ensure that compute capacity is directed toward the most transformative and economically meaningful applications. Designed to be timely and actionable, this session will help you move from reactive allocation to strategic prioritization in an environment defined by rapid technological change.

Enterprises and governments are rethinking how they deploy large foundation models alongside smaller, domain-specific language models to achieve accuracy, security, and cost efficiency at scale. In this session, we explore how Tech Mahindra is leveraging NVIDIA-accelerated platforms to support the evolution from foundation models to production-grade AI factories—systems that continuously train, fine-tune, deploy, and operate both LLMs and SLMs. Drawing on real-world experience, including telecom-specific language models delivering measurable operational outcomes—such as AI-led handling of over 50% of network operations tickets across autonomous and human-approved workflows—and India’s Indus AI mission focused on education at national scale, we show how sovereign AI, private AI, and edge AI can be integrated to deliver secure, scalable, and vertical-ready AI solutions, leveraging NVIDIA technologies such as NVIDIA AI Enterprise, RAPIDS, and NeMo-based data tooling.

In this session, you’ll see how to design and scale multi‑agent systems that combine retrieval, reasoning, and generation to answer complex, enterprise‑grounded queries, while tracking experiments, fine‑tuning models, and building governed, repeatable workflows that can be deployed at scale. ### 从视觉还原到物理交互： 物理可驱动3D 资产生成技术 Beyond Visuals: A Physics-Informed Generative Method for Articulated 3D Assets
Speakers: Dongping Li (字节跳动)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81935/
Type: Talk
本演讲将介绍一种将多模态输入转化为物理可驱动资产的生成式方法。我们将详细阐述如何利用 3D 大模型，从输入图像或自然语言，得到具有完整关节结构的3D资产，涵盖自动化关节估计、部件生成以及物理属性推断等技术。 This session introduces a generative method that transforms multimodal inputs into physically drivable assets. We will detail how we leverage 3D large models to transform images or natural language descriptions into fully articulated assets, featuring automated semantic joint estimation, part-aware mesh generation, and precise physical property inference.

### 基于全局 KV Cache 存储系统的高效 LLM 推理加速方案 Efficient LLM Inference Acceleration via a Global KV Cache Storage System
Speakers: Shunkang Zhang (NVIDIA), Wei Zhang (阿里云智能集团 Alibaba Cloud)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82360/
Type: Talk
KV Cache 通过“以存储换计算”的方式显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）的推理效率。然而，受限于高带宽内存（HBM）容量有限且成本高昂，将 KV Cache 扩展至外部高性能存储系统已成为关键优化方向。 本次 Session 聚焦我们与 NVIDIA 联合开展的系统级协同优化工作，在满足服务等级目标（SLO）的前提下，针对 LLM 推理中动态变化的工作负载，提出一套端到端的全局 KV Cache 解决方案。该方案涵盖：推理引擎与远程存储的深度集成优化，全局 KV Cache 元数据的统一管理与配置策略，面向 KV Cache 访问模式定制的远程存储架构设计。在典型 LLM 推理场景中，该方案实现了显著性能与成本收益：Cache 命中率提升 39%，P99 延迟降低 78%，单位 token 的计算与存储综合成本降至原来的 24%（即降低 76%）。

## Wednesday, March 18

### 基于扩散模型的具身智能：从统一理论到 GPU 加速的机器人策略 Diffusion-Driven Embodied AI: From Unified Theory to GPU-Accelerated Robot Policies
Speakers: 野 石 (上海科技大学，瞬适科技（上海）有限公司), Yu Huang (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81979/
Type: Talk
本报告将系统阐述基于可控扩散模型的新一代机器人学习框架。首先，我们提出一种用于全新的可控扩散模型的统一理论框架，揭示其如何为机器人决策提供一种兼具表达力与可控性的数学新范式。随后，将展示一种突破性的、原生运行于 GPU 的 on-policy 扩散强化学习算法，在效率上树立新的标杆。最后，我们将通过真实的机器人实验展示其在精细操作与运动控制等核心任务上达到的业界领先性能。 This session will systematically elaborate on a new generation robot learning framework based on controllable diffusion models. First, we propose a unified theoretical framework for a novel controllable diffusion model, revealing how it provides a new mathematical paradigm for robot decision-making that combines expressiveness and controllability. Subsequently, a groundbreaking on-policy diffusion-based reinforcement learning algorithm, natively running on GPUs, will be presented, setting a new benchmark in efficiency. Finally, we will demonstrate its industry-leading performance in core tasks such as dexterous manipulation and motion control through real-world robot experiments.

Speakers: Giuditta Zardoni (K2K)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81867/

In this session, we’ll explore how K2K is applying data-compliant Vision-Language Models (VLMs) integrated with NVIDIA Cosmos Reason and Metropolis Blueprint for Video Search and Summarization (VSS) to turn passive footage into proactive decision-making. We’ll walk through how VLM-driven understanding enables cities to automate operational processes, anticipate incidents, and optimize resource allocation through granular semantic insight rather than traditional detection alone. We’ll also share how generated data, synthetic data and simulation pipelines are being used to scale robust urban AI without exposing personal identity data. The result: AI agents that don’t just “see,” but interpret, reason, and support real-time city operations in a trustworthy way.

### Model Builders at the Frontier of EMEA’s Open and Sovereign AI Movement

The discussion will also highlight how NVIDIA Nemotron enables European, Middle Eastern, and African (EMEA) developers to build and fine-tune competitive large models, accelerating innovation while reducing dependence on proprietary platforms. In addition, the panel will touch on how European developers and companies are preparing these models for real-world inference and deployment across diverse applications.

### PAI 集成 NVIDIA Physical AI：驱动数据合成、模型训练测评一体化 PAI Integrates NVIDIA Physical AI: Power E2E SDG, Model Training & Evaluation
Speakers: Boyuan Huang (阿里云)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82282/

自 2025 年，人工智能平台 PAI 融合 NVIDIA Physical AI 领域的多项技术，服务中国及全球的具身智能、自动驾驶及 AGI 大模型团队。此分享将详细介绍 PAI 的 AI 云服务能力和 NVIDIA Physical AI 如何紧密协同，依托 PAI 的高性能计算资源、分布式计算服务以及开箱即用的开发平台，解决数据合成、模型训练、模型测评的实际问题。 同时还将隆重推出 2026 年 PAI & NVIDIA Physical AI 带来的最新升级能力。Since 2025, PAI (Platform for AI) has integrated advanced NVIDIA Physical AI technologies to empower Embodied AI, Autonomous Driving, and AGI teams worldwide. This session provides a technical deep-dive into the seamless synergy between PAI’s cloud-native AI infrastructure and NVIDIA Physical AI, strategically leveraging PAI’s advantages in high-performance computing resource, distributed computing service, and easy-to-use development platform to resolve critical bottlenecks in Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), model training, and performance benchmarking. Furthermore, we will unveil the 2026 roadmap and the latest full-stack feature upgrades.

### Neural Physics for AI-Powered Differentiable PDE-Solvers and Digital Twins
Speakers: Christopher Pain (Imperial College London (ICL))
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81927/
Type: Talk
AI has revolutionized surrogate models in the field of computational physics. However, in this session we take a look at how AI tools can benefit physics-based modeling. We will introduce the neural physics approach, which writes multi-grid solvers as convolutional neural networks whose weights are set from numerical theory, rather than by training. We give demonstrations that indicate the breadth of application possible from complex environmental problems to industrial problems; demonstrate the scalability of the approach; describe the potential for optimization tasks; and discuss future possibilities of combining physics-based models (untrained neural networks from neural physics) with surrogate models (trained neural networks).

This talk will explore how Basler transformed traditional camera hardware into interoperable, scalable virtual models that enable sensor realistic simulation of vision systems and synthetic data generation for AI training.

### A New Paradigm: Verifiable AI
Speakers: Ofir Arkin (NVIDIA), Jonathan Dotan (EQTY Lab), Caroline Yap (Sovereign AI)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81489/
Type: Talk
Autonomous AI requires a new security model. From AI agents to robots, we need to rethink how we should trust these systems to deliver the full promise of their automation. Can we verify their operations, security, and alignment as we grant them access to our most coveted data and delegate them critical tasks?. As multi-agent AI systems span boundaries from cloud to edge, the challenge of maintaining trust and security across every layer of the stack—digital and physical—has never been greater. This session introduces EQTY’s verifiable AI runtime, a new approach that continuously ensures verified integrity from model training and inference to deployment in real-world, physical AI environments, including the verification of AI sovereignty.

### AI Grid Explained: From Secure AI Factories to Distributed Intelligence
Speakers: Sree Sankar (NVIDIA), Masum Mir (Cisco), Shawn Hakl (AT&T)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82010/
Type: Talk
As AI adoption accelerates, isolated AI factories must scale across to a connected, distributed and secure AI grid that can run diverse workloads —from edge AI applications and telco network functions to internal AI services, digital twins, and RAN—while delivering the right performance and efficiency. This session defines what an AI grid is, why it matters, and how to build it. The speakers will walk through its reference architecture, from the compute substrate and high-performance networking to an intelligent, workload-aware control plane and orchestration that spans data centers and sites of all sizes. They will show the types of workloads that are ideal for running on an AI grid and share concrete steps telcos can take to implement a secure AI grid, where to start that journey, and how to unlock new revenue and monetization opportunities today.

### Accelerate the Path to In-Silico Life Sciences and Drug Discovery
Speakers: Anthony Costa (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81663/
Type: Talk
Representing life in silico and designing compounds and therapies that modulate that biology are among the most foundational goals of modern life sciences R&D. The community is at an inflection point: increasing volumes of sophisticated data and foundation modeling efforts require fundamental new technology to accelerate discovery. In this talk, learn about NVIDIA's efforts in addressing these challenges head-on, with new efforts in data generation, data processing, and biological and molecular foundation modeling, and research. We'll cover genomics, multi-omics, foundation model scaling, molecular discovery, and end-to-end AI-scientist workflows in this comprehensive product talk.

### All the Precision, None of the Transistors
Speakers: Harun Bayraktar (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81811/
Type: Talk
We challenge the notion that modern AI-era GPUs are unsuitable for high-precision computations, specifically double-precision (FP64), the cornerstone of scientific computing since 1985. While scalar and vector FP64 throughput in GPUs have continued to improve over generations, Tensor Core throughput for FP64 has not grown at the same rate as reduced-precision operations used by AI. This is alleviated with mixed-precision linear algebra algorithms and Ozaki schemes that utilize reduced-precision Tensor Cores delivering significant performance and efficiency gains while guaranteeing native FP64 accuracy. We will showcase recent advancements in these algorithms, how to use them, and demonstrate their effectiveness in real-world applications. We will also preview an experimental analysis tool designed to improve developer productivity in reasoning about precision choices.

### Best Practices of Multi-Modal and Vision Generation Training in M-Core
Speakers: Junyu Wu (Tencent Holdings Ltd.)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81515/
Type: Talk
Learn our practical strategies for post-training multi-modal vision language models (VLMs) and diffusion models, leveraging M-Core to enhance GPU utilization and improve model performance. Explore our effective reinforcement learning workflow, which spans from training to resource scheduling, maximizing GPU resource efficiency.

### Build Flexible and Secure Power Infrastructure for a Sustainable Future at Scale
Speakers: Varun Sivaram (Emerald AI), Josh Wong (ThinkLabs.ai), Raj Vaswani (ITRON), Shawntia McNeil (NVIDIA), Gabe Mika (Accenture)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81685/
Type: Lightning Talk
To meet the growing demand for power of AI, we have to enable the entire ecosystem, from chip to grid. This lightning talk brings together how NVIDIA is working with its partners from power generation and transmission to distribution to build flexible and reliable infrastructure. This session will focus on use of AI for meeting energy demand, using digital technologies inside data centers to manage peak loads, and applying accelerated computing to simulate the grid and build physical infrastructure.

Speakers: Mayukh Bhaowal (ServiceNow), Julie Black (ServiceNow)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82114/

Effective AI deployment requires more than powerful models—it demands end-to-end governance that spans your entire AI ecosystem. In this session, discover how ServiceNow and NVIDIA collaborate across the full AI stack, from model development and agent deployment to comprehensive governance frameworks. Learn how ServiceNow's AI governance capabilities provide the critical connective tissue connecting your AI investments, enabling you to leverage AI effectively, efficiently, and responsibly. We'll explore industry-specific implementations in financial services, healthcare, technology, and beyond, demonstrating how tailored governance approaches address unique regulatory requirements while accelerating innovation. Built on NVIDIA infrastructure and AI software, this solution ensures your AI initiatives deliver business value while maintaining trust, compliance, and control.

### Building Towards Self-Driving Codebases with Long-Running, Asynchronous Agents
Speakers: Aman Sanger (Cursor)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81528/
Type: Talk
Aman Sanger, co-founder and CTO at Cursor, will share how Cursor is building long-running coding agents that can autonomously execute more ambitious software tasks.

### Computing on the Road: Scaling the Waymo Driver for the Real World
Speakers: Daniel Rosenband (Waymo)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82484/
Type: Talk
Autonomous ride-hailing is increasingly a daily reality for more people with Waymo. As the most mature manifestation of AI in the physical world, Waymo’s path has been defined by 200 million miles and over 20 million trips completed without a human specialist present. Drawing on this unparalleled scale of experience, we will explore Waymo’s approach to developing its own custom hardware, the vital role AI compute plays in enabling it, and how this integrated foundation makes our demonstrably safe AI architecture possible.

### Diffusion Unlocked: Advanced Techniques for Training, Inference, and Deployment
Speakers: Ming-Yu Liu (NVIDIA), Stefano Ermon (Inception Labs), Anastasis Germanidis (Runway), Cynthia Lu (Adobe), Robin Rombach (Black Forest Labs)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81684/
Type: Panel
Diffusion models have exploded from research labs into the mainstream, powering everything from photorealistic image generation to creative co-pilots. But moving these models from a notebook to a scalable, low-latency, production-ready service is one of the greatest engineering challenges in modern AI. The "magic" of generation hides a complex stack of optimization and infrastructure hurdles. We will bypass high-level concepts to debate the concrete architectural trade-offs required for low latency and high throughput, covering unique training challenges, up-and-coming model architectures, inference and runtime optimization, low-precision and NVFP4, and scaling topologies.

### Distributed AI Computing From AI Factories to the Edge (Presented by Supermicro)
Speakers: Thomas Jorgensen (Supermicro), Mory Lin (Supermicro), Steve Stein (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82228/
Type: Talk
Enterprises are moving from AI experimentation to production, where return on investment (ROI) matters as much as performance. This session, presented by Supermicro and NVIDIA, explores how AI factories, edge AI, and on-premises deployments work together to deliver scalable, cost-effective AI solutions. We'll highlight how systems powered by GPUs such as the NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition support inference, visualization, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) workloads across data centers and edge environments. We'll also examine how RAG pipelines benefit from tightly integrated GPUs, networking, and storage to deliver low-latency, context-aware results—helping enterprises improve data control, reduce operational costs, and maximize ROI from AI investments.

### Evaluation-Driven Development: Best Practices for Building Reliable Agents
Speakers: David Austin (NVIDIA), Adel El Hallak (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81706/
Type: Talk
Building agents is easy; reliably improving them is hard. As workflows shift to complex long-horizon tasks, traditional testing fails. How do you debug and improve non-deterministic behavior? This session presents a framework for evaluation-driven development. We move beyond “vibe eval” to disciplined engineering covering ablations, unit tests, trajectory evaluation, and LLM-as-judge patterns. Using a case study of achieving No. 1 on a Deep Research leaderboard, we demonstrate how to build pipelines that catch hallucinations and regressions while preventing overfitting. You'll leave with a workflow for implementing evaluation methods that take agents from prototype to scalable deployment using a suite of tooling enabled by NVIDIA NeMo and open NVIDIA Nemotron models for advanced reasoning.

### Foundation Models Revolutionize Molecular Dynamics Simulations for Chemistry and Materials Science
Speakers: Gabor Csanyi (University of Cambridge)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81802/
Type: Talk
Molecular dynamics simulation, using the explicit equations of quantum mechanics (e.g., density functional theory) is the workhorse of computational materials science and chemistry. Until recently, more than a third of academic supercomputer time was spent on this task. We can now speed up this task by a factor of a million or more using machine learning, without substantial loss of accuracy. This amount of speedup leads to a qualitative change in what is possible to simulate, and therefore to understand, in a wide range of application fields, all the way from catalysis and battery materials to glasses and alloys, and even drug discovery. While some, by now classic, rules of thumb continue to deliver (e.g., more data yield better accuracy), the problem may be special in some ways. We find that encoding the known laws of physics, such as symmetry and locality, help generalization.

### Market Primitives for an Agentic Economy
Speakers: Illia Polosukhin (NEAR)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82411/
Type: Talk
AI agents are emerging as significant economic actors, yet today's markets are designed for human participants, burdened by cognitive load, irrational behavior and biases, and a high-friction legal paradigm. Illia introduces a formal model for agentic markets where autonomous AI agents directly express and match intents. He proposes a new set of agentic market primitives including programmatic escrow, agentic dispute resolution, and intent‑based matching and settlement. This paradigm shift necessitates revising classical economic problems for agentic systems.

You’ll gain valuable insights into how this partnership turned complex challenges into breakthrough results, improving AI infrastructure and accelerating outcomes. Through a customer testimonial journey, we’ll highlight practical lessons, measurable benefits, and how you can apply these insights to your own AI and data center strategies.

### Reimagining Store Operations at Scale to Accelerate Retail ROI
Speakers: Yili Wu (SandStar), Takashi Tokita (Lawson China), Ekaraj Panjavinin (True Corporation Public Co., Ltd.)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81675/

SandStar, Lawson, and True Corp. will explain how to use both NVIDIA edge AI and cloud AI solutions to change thousands of retail stores' daily operations, and attain positive return on investment (ROI) within three to six months.

### Robust LLM Training Infrastructure at ByteDance
Speakers: Zuquan Song (ByteDance Seed)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81421/
Type: Talk
Large language model training has scaled to tens of thousands of GPUs, and keeps expanding. As cluster sizes continually scale, training runs can incur failures, interrupting the run and requiring mitigation. Examples may include NaN values, CUDA errors, and job hangs that challenge stability. High-efficiency LLM training infrastructure requires minimal interruption, fast diagnosis, and strong fault tolerance—we present ByteRobust, a GPU infrastructure management system tailored for robust LLM training. Leveraging LLM training’s parallelism and characteristics, it achieves large-capacity fault tolerance, prompt fault demarcation, and localization via a data-driven approach to ensure continuous efficient training. Deployed on a production GPU platform, ByteRobust sets a new level in training robustness, with 97% effective training time ratio over a three-month training job on 9,600 GPUs.

### Scalable Data Access for Next-Gen Algorithmic Trading
Speakers: Christina Qi (Databento)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s81543/
Type: Talk
Algorithmic trading is entering a new era defined by AI, massive data volumes, and unprecedented compute scale. Many leading firms now operate more like advanced AI labs than traditional trading desks, relying on large fleets of NVIDIA GPUs and consuming petabytes of real-time and historical market data. The main barrier is no longer the models, but accessing and processing the data fast enough to power them. This session traces how the industry has evolved over the past 20 years, where firms are finding real success with AI/ML, and what frontiers are emerging as compute and data converge. We’ll show how new approaches to data delivery, storage, and accelerated networking, together with solutions from Databento and NVIDIA, are opening the door to the next wave of innovation in finance.

### Scaling Deep Tabular: 360K Inferences per Second per GPU
Speakers: Nicolas Chrysanthos (Criteo), Julien Cuquemelle (Criteo)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82431/
Type: Talk
Criteo operates a real-time bidding and recommendation platform that processes over 500 billion requests per day under a strict 20ms latency budget, powered by dozens of production models. This session presents our transition from CPU-based, “classical” machine learning to GPU-accelerated Deep Tabular architectures designed as modular, drop-in replacements, enabling deep learning transition without a full platform rewrite. We will share key architectural choices and dive into inference optimizations required to meet extreme throughput and latency constraints, including client-side batching, Tensor fusion, and resolving bottlenecks in Triton dynamic batching and serving layers. Learn how this transition delivered a 5% business uplift and established the technical foundation for transformer-based, sequence-driven commerce modeling.

page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82018/

Scaling complex models requires infrastructure that evolves with your code. From prototyping to deployment, learn how to optimize your NVIDIA infrastructure for training and inference workloads at scale. Join Erwan Menard, SVP of Product, for a technical look into Crusoe Cloud’s high-performance, energy-first platform and Dhruv Batra, Co-founder and Chief Scientist at Yutori, to hear how they are leveraging Crusoe’s Managed Inference service to re-imagine how people interact on the web In this session, we will deep-dive into Crusoe’s latest capabilities, optimized for the newest NVIDIA GPU architectures. You will learn how Crusoe Cloud will help you navigate the technical demands of massive-scale model training, efficient fine-tuning, and low-latency / high throughput inference. Dhruv will join Erwan to share how Yutori brought their own model to Crusoe’s Managed Inference Service to run agents at scale and they will discuss how to optimize multi-architecture inference for the agentic era. Whether you are an ML engineer optimizing compute resources, a leader scaling production workloads, or a developer delivering the next AI-powered agent or application, this session will show you how to unlock the full potential of NVIDIA compute on Crusoe Cloud.

### Secure Multi-Tenant AI: Sovereign Neo-Cloud Infrastructure With Fractional GPU Sharing
Speakers: Ali Syed (Danish Centre for AI Innovation), Sam Heywood (NVIDIA)
page: https://www.nvidia.com/gtc/session-catalog/sessions/gtc26-s82177/

This session examines how DCAI, a sovereign neo-cloud operator, supports secure multi-tenant access to high-performance AI compute on the Gefion supercomputer. We will show how organizations can share infrastructure while maintaining isolated environments for data, workloads, and networks. The talk covers fractional GPU allocation for efficient resource utilization, pre-configured AI workspaces, and new automation developed with NVIDIA Run:ai that speeds deployment of secure environments. Learn practical approaches for enabling multiple teams to develop and scale AI workloads on shared infrastructure without compromising security or sovereignty.

The conversation will highlight challenges in deploying scalable, safety-critical edge AI and reveal how these global innovators are architecting their next-generation solutions using NVIDIA’s accelerated computing and end-to-end edge platform.
